# Era 1 Expanded Roadmap: v1501-v1600

**Theme:** Development Judgment and Sustained Self-Improvement

This file expands every numbered version and subversion in the era. Browser work is continuous across these planning slots; a numbered checkpoint is a recoverable campaign save, not an automatic Codex handoff. Concrete evidence may justify a narrow repair, deferral, merge, or revised implementation while preserving the era outcome.

# Arc 1: Capability-Value Prioritization

## Section A: Evidence intake (v1501.3-v1508.9)

## v1501: Evidence intake - Scope and evidence baseline

**Arc:** 1, Capability-Value Prioritization  
**Section:** A, Evidence intake  
**Technical dependency label (not handoff cadence):** SHARED  
**Version objective:** Within `Evidence intake`, define the exact observable problem, evidence sources, exclusions, and measurable outcome.  
**Parent outcome:** Add normalized inputs for operator-reported defects, failing tests, diagnostics, performance regressions, conversation-quality findings, missing capabilities, security debt, and structural debt. Preserve provenance and freshness.

### v1501.3 - Integration
- **Owner:** MOBILE-BROWSER CAMPAIGN
- **Deliverable:** For `Evidence intake / Scope and evidence baseline`, Connect the foundation to established project boundaries without creating a second executor, memory store, or authority path.
- **Gate:** Evidence must remain attributable and content-free where public; preserve existing authority, rollback, privacy, and source/runtime boundaries.

### v1501.4 - Operator experience
- **Owner:** MOBILE-BROWSER CAMPAIGN
- **Deliverable:** For `Evidence intake / Scope and evidence baseline`, Add concise controls, status, receipts, correction, cancellation, and accessible presentation for the affected workflow.
- **Gate:** Evidence must remain attributable and content-free where public; preserve existing authority, rollback, privacy, and source/runtime boundaries.

### v1501.5 - Behavioral scenarios
- **Owner:** MOBILE-BROWSER CAMPAIGN
- **Deliverable:** For `Evidence intake / Scope and evidence baseline`, Add realistic end-to-end scenarios, including uncertainty, correction, mixed intent, and ordinary daily use.
- **Gate:** Evidence must remain attributable and content-free where public; preserve existing authority, rollback, privacy, and source/runtime boundaries.

### v1501.6 - Reliability
- **Owner:** MOBILE-BROWSER CAMPAIGN
- **Deliverable:** For `Evidence intake / Scope and evidence baseline`, Harden restart, replay, retry, timeout, cancellation, concurrency, stale state, and exactly-once behavior.
- **Gate:** Evidence must remain attributable and content-free where public; preserve existing authority, rollback, privacy, and source/runtime boundaries.

### v1501.7 - Adversarial review
- **Owner:** MOBILE-BROWSER CAMPAIGN
- **Deliverable:** For `Evidence intake / Scope and evidence baseline`, Test malformed, conflicting, deceptive, private, unauthorized, and scope-expanding inputs. Fail closed with useful guidance.
- **Gate:** Evidence must remain attributable and content-free where public; preserve existing authority, rollback, privacy, and source/runtime boundaries.

### v1501.8 - Reconciliation
- **Owner:** MOBILE-BROWSER CAMPAIGN
- **Deliverable:** For `Evidence intake / Scope and evidence baseline`, Reconcile performance, privacy, compatibility, documentation, release metadata, source immutability, and retained regressions.
- **Gate:** Evidence must remain attributable and content-free where public; preserve existing authority, rollback, privacy, and source/runtime boundaries.

### v1501.9 - Browser checkpoint
- **Owner:** BROWSER-CHECKPOINT (no Codex review required)
- **Deliverable:** For `Evidence intake / Scope and evidence baseline`, Run the complete focused subsection suite and affected retained suites, update the cumulative ledger, and save a recoverable source-only candidate. Continue the campaign unless an exception gate is triggered.
- **Gate:** Evidence must remain attributable and content-free where public; preserve existing authority, rollback, privacy, and source/runtime boundaries.
